4750 — dumbbell russian twist with legs floor off

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4750 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A Russian twist with a dumbbell and your feet lifted off the floor. It strongly challenges the obliques and balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit down and lean back slightly with a dumbbell held in both hands.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit down and lean back slightly with a dumbbell held in both hands. 4750.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your feet off the floor with knees bent. 4750.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rotate your torso to bring the dumbbell beside one hip. 4750.json#/instructions/2
  4. Twist to the other side in a controlled way. 4750.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your chest up and your back long. 4750.json#/instructions/4

4751 — dumbbell glute bridge v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4751 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A glute bridge with a dumbbell resting on your hips. It strengthens the glutes and hamstrings.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with your knees bent and feet flat. Rest a dumbbell on your hips and hold it in place.

4752 — dumbbell side bench squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4752 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A side squat where you sit back toward a bench while holding a dumbbell. It works the thighs and glutes one side at a time.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand sideways to a bench holding a dumbbell at your chest, feet wide.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4752.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4752.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand sideways to a bench holding a dumbbell at your chest, feet wide. 4752.json#/instructions/0
  2. Shift your weight to the leg nearest the bench. 4752.json#/instructions/1
  3. Sit back and down until you lightly touch the bench. 4752.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep the other leg straight. 4752.json#/instructions/3
  5. Drive up through your heel and repeat, then switch sides. 4752.json#/instructions/4

4755 — dumbbell single leg hip thrust

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4755 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A one-leg hip thrust with a dumbbell on your hips. It builds glute strength and hip stability.

Proposed staging

Starting Position
